DECLARE
   vALertId  		ALERT;
	 vAlertButton  NUMBER;		
   IF :SYSTEM.MODE = 'NORMAL' THEN
      IF :SYSTEM.FORM_STATUS = 'CHANGED' 
  	     AND GET_ITEM_PROPERTY('TOOLBAR.SAVE',ENABLED) = 'TRUE' THEN	
	       vALertId := FIND_ALERT('TWOBUTTON');
	       IF NOT ID_NULL(vALertId) THEN
	   	      -- Set Alert Text ( Title,Message,Button(s) )
            SET_ALERT_PROPERTY(vALertId,TITLE,'Choices/????????');
            SET_ALERT_PROPERTY(vALertId,ALERT_MESSAGE_TEXT,'Save Changes/??? ??????? ?');
            SET_ALERT_BUTTON_PROPERTY(vALertId,ALERT_BUTTON1,LABEL,'Yes/???');
            SET_ALERT_BUTTON_PROPERTY(vALertId,ALERT_BUTTON2,LABEL,'No/??');
           -- Get Return Value
    	      vAlertButton := SHOW_ALERT(vALertId);
    	      -- Perform Action Needed per return value
	          IF    vAlertButton = ALERT_BUTTON1 THEN
	   	          DO_KEY('COMMIT_FORM');
	          END IF;	   	    	   
	       END IF;
	    END IF;   
	    CLEAR_FORM(NO_VALIDATE);  
	    :TOOLBAR.UFORMNAME := :SYSTEM.CURRENT_FORM;	    
   	  SET_ITEM_PROPERTY('TOOLBAR.QUERY',LABEL,'Ex.Query');
   	  ENTER_QUERY;
   ELSE
   	  SET_ITEM_PROPERTY('TOOLBAR.QUERY',LABEL,'Query');   	
   	  EXECUTE_QUERY;
   END IF;		 
#WHEN-BUTTON-PRESSED (TOOLBAR.QUERY)
STANDARD
